method: per file, read ONLY the first <bytes> bytes recorded in logfreeze-01-manifest.txt,
        then keep matching lines plus 3 lines of context. Whatever this extraction itself
        causes to be logged lands past the cut and cannot appear below. No timestamp
        parsing is used anywhere - byte offsets are exact, timestamps are not.
redact: line-granular and unconditional; a redacted line keeps its path:line prefix.
